# Skills · A · part 70

973 entries, ai-eval-ci to ai-gateway-architect. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ai-eval-ci**: Run AI agent and LLM evaluations in CI/CD pipelines — automated quality gates that fail the build when AI output quality drops. Use when someone asks to "test my AI agent", "add evals to CI", "catch prompt regressions", "compare models", "evaluate LLM output quality", "set up AI quality gates", or "benchmark my agent before deploying". Covers eval frameworks (Cobalt, Promptfoo, Braintrust), LLM-as-judge scoring, threshold-based assertions, and GitHub Actions integration. ([TerminalSkills/skills](https://github.com/TerminalSkills/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/ai-eval-ci))

- **ai-evaluation-harness**: MANUAL-ONLY; never auto-invoke. Design and run the evaluation harness for a large language model (LLM) feature: a versioned dataset, quality and safety graders, pass/fail thresholds, and a continuous integration (CI) gate that blocks regressions. Include representative, adversarial, and regression cases; measure schema, refusal, grounding, injection resistance, latency, and cost.
